- **harness-import**: Read how an installed copy of this harness was modified locally, judge which edits generalize, and propose them as changes to the harness payload. Use when a user asks why an agent failed or underperformed on a harness project, when a session ends with correction_cycles &gt;= 3 on any feature, or when the user says "improve the harness".
